<sup>19</sup> He appointed the moon for seasons:<br>The sun knoweth his going down.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Thou makest darkness, and it is night,<br>Wherein all the beasts of the forest creep forth.<br>
<sup>21</sup> The young lions roar after their prey,<br>And seek their food from God.<br>
<sup>22</sup> The sun ariseth, they get them away,<br>And lay them down in their dens.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Man goeth forth unto his work<br>And to his labor until the evening.<br>
<sup>24</sup> O Jehovah, how manifold are thy works!<br>In wisdom hast thou made them all:<br>The earth is full of thy <sup>h</sup>riches.<br>
<sup>25</sup> Yonder is the sea, great and wide,<br>Wherein are things creeping innumerable,<br>Both small and great beasts.<br>
<sup>26</sup> There go the ships;<br>There is leviathan, whom thou hast formed to play <sup>i</sup>therein.<br>
<sup>27</sup> These wait all for thee,<br>That thou mayest give them their food in due season.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Thou givest unto them, they gather;<br>Thou openest thy hand, they are satisfied with good.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Thou hidest thy face, they are troubled;<br>Thou <sup>j</sup>takest away their breath, they die,<br>And return to their dust.<br>
<sup>30</sup> Thou sendest forth thy Spirit, they are created;<br>And thou renewest the face of the ground.<br>
<sup>31</sup> Let the glory of Jehovah endure for ever;<br>Let Jehovah rejoice in his works:<br>
<sup>32</sup> Who looketh on the earth, and it trembleth;<br>He toucheth the mountains, and they smoke.<br>
<sup>33</sup> I will sing unto Jehovah as long as I live:<br>I will sing praise to my God while I have any being.<br>
<sup>34</sup> Let my meditation be sweet unto him:<br>I will rejoice in Jehovah.<br>
<sup>35</sup> Let sinners be consumed out of the earth.<br>And let the wicked be no more.<br>Bless Jehovah, O my soul.<sup>k</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, _his angels winds_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Hebrew _He founded the earth upon its bases_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Or, _They went up by the mountains, they went down by the valleys_
> - <sup>d</sup> 12. Hebrew _utter their voice_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 14. Or, _labor_
> - <sup>f</sup> 14. Hebrew _bread_.
> - <sup>g</sup> 15. Hebrew _To make his face to shine with oil_.
> - <sup>h</sup> 24. Or, _creatures_
> - <sup>i</sup> 26. Or, _with him_. See Job 41:5.
> - <sup>j</sup> 29. Or, _gatherest in_
> - <sup>k</sup> 35. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 105

### Jehovah’s wonderful works in behalf of Israel.

<sup>1</sup> Oh give thanks unto Jehovah, call upon his name;<br>Make known among the peoples his doings.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Sing unto him, sing praises unto him;<sup>a</sup><br>Talk ye of all his marvellous works.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Glory ye in his holy name:<br>Let the heart of them rejoice that seek Jehovah.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Seek ye Jehovah and his strength;<br>Seek his face evermore.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Remember his marvellous works that he hath done,<br>His wonders, and the judgments of his mouth,<br>
<sup>6</sup> O ye seed of Abraham his servant,<br>Ye children of Jacob, his chosen ones.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He is Jehovah our God:<br>His judgments are in all the earth.<br>
<sup>8</sup> He hath remembered his covenant for ever,<br>The word which he commanded to a thousand generations,<br>
<sup>9</sup> _The covenant_ which he made with Abraham,<br>And his oath unto Isaac,<br>
<sup>10</sup> And confirmed the same unto Jacob for a statute,<br>To Israel for an everlasting covenant,<br>
<sup>11</sup> Saying, Unto thee will I give the land of Canaan,<br>The <sup>b</sup>lot of your inheritance;<br>
<sup>12</sup> When they were but a few men in number,<br>Yea, very few, and sojourners in it.<br>
<sup>13</sup> And they went about from nation to nation,<br>From one kingdom to another people.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He suffered no man to do them wrong;<br>Yea, he reproved kings for their sakes,<br>
<sup>15</sup> _Saying_, Touch not mine anointed ones,<br>And do my prophets no harm.<br>
<sup>16</sup> And he called for a famine upon the land;<br>He brake the whole staff of bread.<br>
<sup>17</sup> He sent a man before them;<br>Joseph was sold for a servant:<br>
<sup>18</sup> His feet they hurt with fetters:<sup>c</sup><br>He was laid in _chains of_ iron,<br>
<sup>19</sup> Until the time that his word came to pass,<br>The word of Jehovah tried him.<br>
<sup>20</sup> The king sent and loosed him;<br>Even the ruler of peoples, and let him go free.<br>
<sup>21</sup> He made him lord of his house,<br>And ruler of all his substance;<br>
<sup>22</sup> To bind his princes at his pleasure,<br>And teach his elders wisdom.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Israel also came into Egypt;<br>And Jacob sojourned in the land of Ham.<br>
<sup>24</sup> And he increased his people greatly,<br>And made them stronger than their adversaries.<br>
<sup>25</sup> He turned their heart to hate his people,<br>To deal subtly with his servants.<br>
<sup>26</sup> He sent Moses his servant,<br>_And_ Aaron whom he had chosen.<br>
<sup>27</sup> They set among them <sup>d</sup>his signs,<br>And wonders in the land of Ham.<br>
<sup>28</sup> He sent darkness, and made it dark;<br>And they rebelled not against his words.<br>
<sup>29</sup> He turned their waters into blood,<br>And slew their fish.<br>
<sup>30</sup> Their land swarmed with frogs<br>In the chambers of their kings.<br>
<sup>31</sup> He spake, and there came swarms of flies,<br>And lice in all their borders.<br>
<sup>32</sup> He gave them hail for rain,<br>_And_ flaming fire in their land.<br>
<sup>33</sup> He smote their vines also and their fig-trees,<br>And brake the trees of their borders.<br>
<sup>34</sup> He spake, and the locust came,<br>And the grasshopper, and that without number,<br>
<sup>35</sup> And did eat up every herb in their land,<br>And did eat up the fruit of their ground.<br>
<sup>36</sup> He smote also all the first-born in their land,<br>The <sup>e</sup>chief of all their strength.<br>
<sup>37</sup> And he brought them forth with silver and gold;<br>And there was <sup>f</sup>not one feeble person among his tribes.<br>
<sup>38</sup> Egypt was glad when they departed;<br>For the fear of them had fallen upon them.<br>
<sup>39</sup> He spread a cloud for a covering,<br>And fire to give light in the night.<br>
<sup>40</sup> They asked, and he brought quails,<br>And satisfied them with the bread of heaven.<br>
<sup>41</sup> He opened the rock, and waters gushed out;<br>They ran in the dry places _like_ a river.<br>
<sup>42</sup> For he remembered his holy word,<br>_And_ Abraham his servant.<br>
<sup>43</sup> And he brought forth his people with joy,<br>_And_ his chosen with singing.<br>
<sup>44</sup> And he gave them the lands of the nations;<br>And they took the labor of the peoples in possession:<br>
<sup>45</sup> That they might keep his statutes,<br>And observe his laws.<sup>g</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _Meditate_
> - <sup>b</sup> 11. Hebrew _cord_, or _line_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 18. Hebrew _His soul entered into the iron_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 27. Hebrew _the words of his signs_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 36. Hebrew _beginning_. See Dt. 21:17.
> - <sup>f</sup> 37. Or, _none that stumbled_
> - <sup>g</sup> 45. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 106

### Israel’s rebelliousness and Jehovah’s deliverances.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Oh give thanks unto Jehovah; for he is <sup>a</sup>good;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Who can utter the mighty acts of Jehovah,<br>Or show forth all his praise?<br>
<sup>3</sup> Blessed are they that keep justice,<br>And he that doeth righteousness at all times.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Remember me, O Jehovah, with the favor that thou bearest unto thy people;<br>Oh visit me with thy salvation,<br>
<sup>5</sup> That I may see the prosperity of thy chosen,<br>That I may rejoice in the gladness of thy nation,<br>That I may glory with thine inheritance.<br>
<sup>6</sup> We have sinned with our fathers,<br>We have committed iniquity, we have done wickedly.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Our fathers understood not thy wonders in Egypt;<br>They remembered not the multitude of thy lovingkindnesses,<br>But were rebellious at the sea, even at the Red Sea.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Nevertheless he saved them for his name’s sake,<br>That he might make his mighty power to be known.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He rebuked the Red Sea also, and it was dried up:<br>So he led them through the depths, as through a <sup>b</sup>wilderness.<br>
<sup>10</sup> And he saved them from the hand of him that hated them,<br>And redeemed them from the hand of the enemy.<br>
<sup>11</sup> And the waters covered their adversaries;<br>There was not one of them left.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Then believed they his words;<br>They sang his praise.<br>
<sup>13</sup> They soon forgat his works;<br>They waited not for his counsel,<br>
<sup>14</sup> But lusted exceedingly in the wilderness,<br>And tempted God in the desert.<br>
<sup>15</sup> And he gave them their request,<br>But sent leanness into their soul.<br>
<sup>16</sup> They envied Moses also in the camp,<br>_And_ Aaron the <sup>c</sup>saint of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>17</sup> The earth opened and swallowed up Dathan,<br>And covered the company of Abiram.<br>
<sup>18</sup> And a fire was kindled in their company;<br>The flame burned up the wicked.<br>
<sup>19</sup> They made a calf in Horeb,<br>And worshipped a molten image.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Thus they changed their glory<br>For the likeness of an ox that eateth grass.<br>
<sup>21</sup> They forgat God their Saviour,<br>Who had done great things in Egypt,<br>
<sup>22</sup> Wondrous works in the land of Ham,<br>_And_ terrible things by the Red Sea.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Therefore he said that he would destroy them,<br>Had not Moses his chosen stood before him in the breach,<br>To turn away his wrath, lest he should destroy _them_.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Yea, they despised the pleasant land,<br>They believed not his word,<br>
<sup>25</sup> But murmured in their tents,<br>And hearkened not unto the voice of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Therefore he <sup>d</sup>sware unto them,<br>That he would <sup>e</sup>overthrow them in the wilderness,<br>
<sup>27</sup> And that he would overthrow their seed among the nations,<br>And scatter them in the lands.<br>
<sup>28</sup> They joined themselves also unto Baal-peor,<br>And ate the sacrifices of the dead.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Thus they provoked him to anger with their doings;<br>And the plague brake in upon them.<br>
<sup>30</sup> Then stood up Phinehas, and executed judgment;<br>And so the plague was stayed.<br>
<sup>31</sup> And that was reckoned unto him for righteousness,<br>Unto all generations for evermore.<br>
<sup>32</sup> They angered him also at the waters of <sup>f</sup>Meribah,<br>So that it went ill with Moses for their sakes;<br>
<sup>33</sup> Because they were rebellious against his spirit,<br>And he spake unadvisedly with his lips.<br>
<sup>34</sup> They did not destroy the peoples,<br>As Jehovah commanded them,<br>
<sup>35</sup> But mingled themselves with the nations,<br>And learned their works,<br>
<sup>36</sup> And served their idols,<br>Which became a snare unto them.<br>
<sup>37</sup> Yea, they sacrificed their sons and their daughters unto demons,<br>
<sup>38</sup> And shed innocent blood,<br>Even the blood of their sons and of their daughters,<br>Whom they sacrificed unto the idols of Canaan;<br>And the land was polluted with blood.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Thus were they defiled with their works,<br>And played the harlot in their doings.<br>
<sup>40</sup> Therefore was the wrath of Jehovah kindled against his people,<br>And he abhorred his inheritance.<br>
<sup>41</sup> And he gave them into the hand of the nations;<br>And they that hated them ruled over them.<br>
<sup>42</sup> Their enemies also oppressed them,<br>And they were brought into subjection under their hand.<br>
<sup>43</sup> Many times did he deliver them;<br>But they were rebellious in their counsel,<br>And were brought low in their iniquity.<br>
<sup>44</sup> Nevertheless he regarded their distress,<br>When he heard their cry:<br>
<sup>45</sup> And he remembered for them his covenant,<br>And repented according to the multitude of his lovingkindnesses.<br>
<sup>46</sup> He made them also to be pitied<br>Of all those that carried them captive.<br>
<sup>47</sup> Save us, O Jehovah our God,<br>And gather us from among the nations,<br>To give thanks unto thy holy name,<br>And to triumph in thy praise.<br>
<sup>48</sup> Blessed be Jehovah, the God of Israel,<br>From everlasting even to everlasting.<br>And let all the people say, Amen.<sup>g</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 9. Or, _pasture-land_
> - <sup>c</sup> 16. Or, _holy one_
> - <sup>d</sup> 26. Hebrew _lifted up his hand_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 26. Hebrew _make them fall_.
> - <sup>f</sup> 32. Or, _strife_
> - <sup>g</sup> 48. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 107

### Jehovah delivers men from manifold troubles.

<sup>1</sup> O give thanks unto Jehovah; for he is good;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let the redeemed of Jehovah say _so_,<br>Whom he hath redeemed from the hand of the adversary,<br>
<sup>3</sup> And gathered out of the lands,<br>From the east and from the west,<br>From the north and <sup>a</sup>from the south.<br>
<sup>4</sup> They wandered in the wilderness in a desert way;<br>They found no city of habitation.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Hungry and thirsty,<br>Their soul fainted in them.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Then they cried unto Jehovah in their trouble,<br>And he delivered them out of their distresses,<br>
<sup>7</sup> He led them also by a straight way,<br>That they might go to a city of habitation.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Oh that men would praise Jehovah for his lovingkindness,<br>And for his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>9</sup> For he satisfieth the longing soul,<br>And the hungry soul he filleth with good.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Such as sat in darkness and in the shadow of death,<br>Being bound in affliction and iron,<br>
<sup>11</sup> Because they rebelled against the words of God,<br>And contemned the counsel of the Most High:<br>
<sup>12</sup> Therefore he brought down their heart with labor;<br>They fell down, and there was none to help.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Then they cried unto Jehovah in their trouble,<br>And he saved them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He brought them out of darkness and the shadow of death,<br>And brake their bonds in sunder.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Oh that men would praise Jehovah for his lovingkindness,<br>And for his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>16</sup> For he hath broken the gates of brass,<br>And cut the bars of iron in sunder.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Fools because of <sup>b</sup>their transgression,<br>And because of their iniquities, are afflicted.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Their soul abhorreth all manner of food;<br>And they draw near unto the gates of death.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Then they cry unto Jehovah in their trouble,<br>And he saveth them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>20</sup> He sendeth his word, and healeth them,<br>And delivereth _them_ from their <sup>c</sup>destructions.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Oh that men would praise Jehovah for his lovingkindness,<br>And for his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>22</sup> And let them offer the sacrifices of thanksgiving,<br>And declare his works with singing.<br>
<sup>23</sup> They that go down to the sea in ships,<br>That do business in great waters;<br>
<sup>24</sup> These see the works of Jehovah,<br>And his wonders in the deep.<br>
<sup>25</sup> For he commandeth, and raiseth the stormy wind,<br>Which lifteth up the waves thereof.<br>
<sup>26</sup> They mount up to the heavens, they go down again to the depths:<br>Their soul melteth away because of trouble.<br>
<sup>27</sup> They reel to and fro, and stagger like a drunken man,<br>And <sup>d</sup>are at their wits’ end.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Then they cry unto Jehovah in their trouble,<br>And he bringeth them out of their distresses.<br>
<sup>29</sup> He maketh the storm a calm,<br>So that the waves thereof are still.<br>
<sup>30</sup> Then are they glad because they are quiet;<br>So he bringeth them unto their desired haven.<br>
<sup>31</sup> Oh that men would praise Jehovah for his lovingkindness,<br>And for his wonderful works to the children of men!<br>
<sup>32</sup> Let them exalt him also in the assembly of the people,<br>And praise him in the seat of the elders.<br>
<sup>33</sup> He turneth rivers into a wilderness,<br>And watersprings into a thirsty ground;<br>
<sup>34</sup> A fruitful land into a salt desert,<br>For the wickedness of them that dwell therein.<br>
<sup>35</sup> He turneth a wilderness into a pool of water,<br>And a dry land into watersprings.<br>
<sup>36</sup> And there he maketh the hungry to dwell,<br>That they may prepare a city of habitation,<br>
<sup>37</sup> And sow fields, and plant vineyards,<br>And get them fruits of increase.<br>
<sup>38</sup> He blesseth them also, so that they are multiplied greatly;<br>And he suffereth not their cattle to decrease.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Again, they are diminished and bowed down<br>Through oppression, trouble, and sorrow.<br>
<sup>40</sup> He poureth contempt upon princes,<br>And causeth them to wander in the waste, where there is no way.<br>
<sup>41</sup> Yet setteth he the needy on high from affliction,<br>And maketh _him_ families like a flock.<br>
<sup>42</sup> The upright shall see it, and be glad;<br>And all iniquity shall stop her mouth.<br>
<sup>43</sup> Whoso is wise will give heed to these things;<br>And they will consider the lovingkindnesses of Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Hebrew _from the sea_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 17. Hebrew _the way of their transgression_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 20. Hebrew _pits_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 27. Hebrew _all their wisdom is swallowed up_.

## Chapter 108

### God praised, and supplicated to give victory.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song, a Psalm of David._My heart is fixed, O God;<br>I will sing, yea, I will sing praises, even with my glory.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Awake, psaltery and harp:<br>I myself will <sup>a</sup>awake right early.<br>
<sup>3</sup> I will give thanks unto thee, O Jehovah, among the peoples;<br>And I will sing praises unto thee among the nations.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For thy lovingkindness is great above the heavens;<br>And thy truth _reacheth_ unto the skies.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Be thou exalted, O God, above the heavens,<br>And thy glory above all the earth.<br>
<sup>6</sup> That thy beloved may be delivered,<br>Save with thy right hand, and answer <sup>b</sup>us.<br>
<sup>7</sup> God hath spoken in his holiness: I will exult;<br>I will divide Shechem, and mete out the valley of Succoth.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Gilead is mine; Manasseh is mine;<br>Ephraim also is the defence of my head;<br>Judah is my <sup>c</sup>sceptre.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Moab is my washpot;<sup>d</sup><br>Upon Edom will I cast my shoe;<br>Over Philistia will I shout.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Who will bring me into the fortified city?<br>Who <sup>e</sup>hath led me unto Edom?<br>
<sup>11</sup> <sup>f</sup>Hast not thou cast us off, O God?<br>And thou goest not forth, O God, with our hosts.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Give us help against the adversary;<br>For vain is the <sup>g</sup>help of man.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Through God we shall do valiantly:<br>For he it is that will tread down our adversaries.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _awake the dawn_
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Another reading is, _me_
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Or, _lawgiver_
> - <sup>d</sup> 9. Or, _Unto_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Or, _will lead me etc._
> - <sup>f</sup> 11. Or, _Wilt not thou, O God, who hast cast us off, And goest . . . hosts?_
> - <sup>g</sup> 12. Hebrew _salvation_.

## Chapter 109

### Vengeance invoked upon adversaries.

<sup>1</sup> > _For the Chief Musician. A Psalm of David._Hold not thy peace, O God of my praise;<br>
<sup>2</sup> For the mouth of the wicked and the mouth of deceit have they opened against me:<br>They have spoken <sup>a</sup>unto me with a lying tongue.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They have compassed me about also with words of hatred,<br>And fought against me without a cause.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For my love they are my adversaries:<br>But I _give myself unto_ prayer.<br>
<sup>5</sup> And they have <sup>b</sup>rewarded me evil for good,<br>And hatred for my love.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Set thou a wicked man over him;<br>And let <sup>c</sup>an adversary stand at his right hand.<br>
<sup>7</sup> When he is judged, let him come forth guilty;<br>And let his prayer <sup>d</sup>be turned into sin.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Let his days be few;<br>_And_ let another take his office.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Let his children be fatherless,<br>And his wife a widow.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let his children be vagabonds, and beg;<br>And let them seek _their bread_<sup>e</sup> out of their desolate places.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Let the extortioner <sup>f</sup>catch all that he hath;<br>And let strangers make spoil of his labor.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Let there be none to <sup>g</sup>extend kindness unto him;<br>Neither let there be any to have pity on his fatherless children.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Let his posterity be cut off;<br>In the generation following let their name be blotted out.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Let the iniquity of his fathers be remembered with Jehovah;<br>And let not the sin of his mother be blotted out.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Let them be before Jehovah continually,<br>That he may cut off the memory of them from the earth;<br>
<sup>16</sup> Because he remembered not to show kindness,<br>But persecuted the poor and needy man,<br>And the broken in heart, to slay _them_.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Yea, he loved cursing, and it came unto him;<br>And he delighted not in blessing, and it was far from him.<br>
<sup>18</sup> He clothed himself also with cursing as with his garment,<br>And it came into his inward parts like water,<br>And like oil into his bones.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Let it be unto him as the raiment wherewith he covereth himself,<br>And for the girdle wherewith he is girded continually.<br>
<sup>20</sup> This is the reward of mine adversaries from Jehovah,<br>And of them that speak evil against my soul.<br>
<sup>21</sup> But deal thou with me, O Jehovah the Lord, for thy name’s sake:<br>Because thy lovingkindness is good, deliver thou me;<br>
<sup>22</sup> For I am poor and needy,<br>And my heart is wounded within me.<br>
<sup>23</sup> I am gone like the shadow when it <sup>h</sup>declineth:<br>I am tossed up and down as the locust.<br>
<sup>24</sup> My knees <sup>i</sup>are weak through fasting;<br>And my flesh faileth of fatness.<br>
<sup>25</sup> I am become also a reproach unto them:<br>When they see me, they shake their head.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Help me, O Jehovah my God;<br>Oh save me according to thy lovingkindness:<br>
<sup>27</sup> That they may know that this is thy hand;<br>_That_ thou, Jehovah, hast done it.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Let them curse, but bless thou:<br>When they arise, they shall be put to shame,<br>But thy servant shall rejoice.<br>
<sup>29</sup> <sup>j</sup>Let mine adversaries be clothed with dishonor,<br>And let them cover themselves with their own shame as with a robe.<br>
<sup>30</sup> I will give great thanks unto Jehovah with my mouth;<br>Yea, I will praise him among the multitude.<br>
<sup>31</sup> For he will stand at the right hand of the needy,<br>To save him from them that judge his soul.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _against_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Hebrew _laid upon me_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Or, _Satan_. Or, _an accuser_
> - <sup>d</sup> 7. Or, _become_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Or, _far from_
> - <sup>f</sup> 11. Hebrew _snare_.
> - <sup>g</sup> 12. Or, _continue_
> - <sup>h</sup> 23. Or, _is stretched out_
> - <sup>i</sup> 24. Or, _totter_
> - <sup>j</sup> 29. Or, _Mine adversaries shall be clothed . . . And they shall cover etc._

## Chapter 110

### Jehovah gives dominion to the king.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Psalm of David._Jehovah saith unto my Lord, Sit thou at my right hand,<br>Until I make thine enemies thy footstool.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Jehovah will <sup>a</sup>send forth the <sup>b</sup>rod of thy strength out of Zion:<br>Rule thou in the midst of thine enemies.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thy people <sup>c</sup>offer themselves willingly<br>In the day of thy <sup>d</sup>power, <sup>e</sup>in holy array:<br>Out of the womb of the morning<sup>f</sup><br>Thou hast the dew of thy youth.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Jehovah hath sworn, and will not repent:<br>Thou art a priest for ever<br>After the <sup>g</sup>order of Melchizedek.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The Lord at thy right hand<sup>h</sup><br>Will strike through kings in the day of his wrath.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He will judge among the nations,<sup>i</sup><br>He <sup>j</sup>will fill _the places_ with dead bodies;<br>He <sup>k</sup>will strike through the head <sup>l</sup>in many countries.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He will drink of the brook in the way:<br>Therefore will he lift up the head.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _stretch_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Or, _sceptre_
> - <sup>c</sup> 3. Hebrew _are freewill-offerings_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 3. Or, _army_
> - <sup>e</sup> 3. Or, _in the beauty of holiness_
> - <sup>f</sup> 3. Or, _Thy youth are to thee_ as _the dew_
> - <sup>g</sup> 4. Or, _manner_
> - <sup>h</sup> 5. Or, _Hath stricken_
> - <sup>i</sup> 6. Or, The places _are full of etc._
> - <sup>j</sup> 6. Or, _hath filled_
> - <sup>k</sup> 6. Or, _Hath stricken_
> - <sup>l</sup> 6. Or, _over a wide land_

## Chapter 111

### Jehovah praised for his goodness.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>I will give than<sup>a</sup>ks unto Jehovah with my whole heart,<br>In the council of the upright, and in the congregation.<br>
<sup>2</sup> The works of Jehovah are great,<br>Sought out of all them that have pleasure therein.<br>
<sup>3</sup> His work is honor and majesty;<br>And his righteousness endureth for ever.<br>
<sup>4</sup> He hath made his wonderful works to be remembered:<br>Jehovah is gracious and merciful.<br>
<sup>5</sup> He hath given <sup>b</sup>food unto them that fear him:<br>He will ever be mindful of his covenant.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He hath showed his people the power of his works,<br>In giving them the heritage of the nations.<br>
<sup>7</sup> The works of his hands are truth and justice;<br>All his precepts are sure.<br>
<sup>8</sup> They are established for ever and ever;<br>They are <sup>c</sup>done in truth and uprightness.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He hath sent redemption unto his people;<br>He hath commanded his covenant for ever:<br>Holy and reverend is his name.<br>
<sup>10</sup> The fear of Jehovah is the beginning of wisdom;<sup>d</sup><br>A good understanding have all they that do <sup>e</sup>_his commandments_:<br>His praise endureth for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Hebrew _prey_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Or, _made_
> - <sup>d</sup> 10. Or, _Good repute_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Hebrew _them_.

## Chapter 112

### Prosperity of him that fears Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Blessed is the man t<sup>a</sup>hat feareth Jehovah,<br>That delighteth greatly in his commandments.<br>
<sup>2</sup> His seed shall be mighty upon earth:<br>The generation of the upright shall be blessed.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Wealth and riches are in his house;<br>And his righteousness endureth for ever.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Unto the upright there ariseth light in the darkness:<br>_He is_ gracious, and merciful, and righteous.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Well is it with the man that dealeth graciously and lendeth;<br>He shall maintain his cause in judgment.<br>
<sup>6</sup> For he shall never be moved;<br>The righteous shall be had in everlasting remembrance.<br>
<sup>7</sup> He shall not be afraid of evil tidings:<br>His heart is fixed, trusting in Jehovah.<br>
<sup>8</sup> His heart is established, he shall not be afraid,<br>Until he see _his desire_ upon his adversaries.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He hath dispersed, he hath given to the needy;<br>His righteousness endureth for ever:<br>His horn shall be exalted with honor.<br>
<sup>10</sup> The wicked shall see it, and be grieved;<br>He shall gnash with his teeth, and melt away:<br>The desire of the wicked shall perish.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 113

### Jehovah praised for exalting the humble.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Praise, O ye servants o<sup>a</sup>f Jehovah,<br>Praise the name of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Blessed be the name of Jehovah<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>
<sup>3</sup> From the rising of the sun unto the going down of the same<br>Jehovah’s name is to be praised.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Jehovah is high above all nations,<br>And his glory above the heavens.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Who is like unto Jehovah our God,<br>That hath his seat on high,<br>
<sup>6</sup> That humbleth himself <sup>b</sup>to behold<br>_The things that are_ in heaven and in the earth?<br>
<sup>7</sup> He raiseth up the poor out of the dust,<br>And lifteth up the needy from the dunghill;<br>
<sup>8</sup> That he may set him with princes,<br>Even with the princes of his people.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He maketh the barren woman to keep house,<br>_And to be_ a joyful mother of children.<sup>c</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Or, _to regard the heavens and the earth_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 114

### God’s deliverance of Israel from Egypt.

<sup>1</sup> When Israel went forth out of Egypt,<br>The house of Jacob from a people of strange language;<br>
<sup>2</sup> Judah became his sanctuary,<br>Israel his dominion.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The sea saw it, and fled;<br>The Jordan was driven back.<br>
<sup>4</sup> The mountains skipped like rams,<br>The little hills like lambs.<br>
<sup>5</sup> What aileth thee, O thou sea, that thou fleest?<br>Thou Jordan, that thou turnest back?<br>
<sup>6</sup> Ye mountains, that ye skip like rams;<br>Ye little hills, like lambs?<br>
<sup>7</sup> Tremble, thou earth, at the presence of the Lord,<br>At the presence of the God of Jacob,<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who turned the rock into a pool of water,<br>The flint into a fountain of waters.<br>

## Chapter 115

### Heathen idols contrasted with Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> Not unto us, O Jehovah, not unto us,<br>But unto thy name give glory,<br>For thy lovingkindness, and for thy truth’s sake.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Wherefore should the nations say,<br>Where is now their God?<br>
<sup>3</sup> But our God is in the heavens:<br>He hath done whatsoever he pleased.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Their idols are silver and gold,<br>The work of men’s hands.<br>
<sup>5</sup> They have mouths, but they speak not;<br>Eyes have they, but they see not;<br>
<sup>6</sup> They have ears, but they hear not;<br>Noses have they, but they smell not;<br>
<sup>7</sup> They have hands, but they handle not;<br>Feet have they, but they walk not;<br>Neither speak they through their throat.<br>
<sup>8</sup> They that make them shall be like unto them;<br>Yea, every one that trusteth in them.<br>
<sup>9</sup> O Israel, trust thou in Jehovah:<br>He is their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>10</sup> O house of Aaron, trust ye in Jehovah:<br>He is their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Ye that fear Jehovah, trust in Jehovah:<br>He is their help and their shield.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Jehovah hath been mindful of us; he will bless _us_:<br>He will bless the house of Israel;<br>He will bless the house of Aaron.<br>
<sup>13</sup> He will bless them that fear Jehovah,<br>Both small and great.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Jehovah increase you more and more,<br>You and your children.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Blessed are ye of Jehovah,<br>Who made heaven and earth.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The heavens are the heavens of Jehovah;<br>But the earth hath he given to the children of men.<br>
<sup>17</sup> The dead praise not <sup>a</sup>Jehovah,<br>Neither any that go down into silence;<br>
<sup>18</sup> But we will bless <sup>b</sup>Jehovah<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<sup>c</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 17. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 18. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 18. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 116

### Thanksgiving for deliverance from death.

<sup>1</sup> I love Jehovah, because he heareth<br>My voice and my supplications.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Because he hath inclined his ear unto me,<br>Therefore will I call _upon him_ as long as I live.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The cords of death compassed me,<br>And the pains of Sheol <sup>a</sup>gat hold upon me:<br>I found trouble and sorrow.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Then called I upon the name of Jehovah:<br>O Jehovah, I beseech thee, deliver my soul.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Gracious is Jehovah, and righteous;<br>Yea, our God is merciful.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Jehovah preserveth the simple:<br>I was brought low, and he saved me.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Return unto thy rest, O my soul;<br>For Jehovah hath dealt bountifully with thee.<br>
<sup>8</sup> For thou hast delivered my soul from death,<br>Mine eyes from tears,<br>_And_ my feet from falling.<br>
<sup>9</sup> I will walk before Jehovah<br>In the <sup>b</sup>land of the living.<br>
<sup>10</sup> I <sup>c</sup>believe, for I will speak:<br>I was greatly afflicted:<br>
<sup>11</sup> I said in my <sup>d</sup>haste,<br>All men are liars.<br>
<sup>12</sup> What shall I render unto Jehovah<br>For all his benefits toward me?<br>
<sup>13</sup> I will take the cup of salvation,<br>And call upon the name of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I will pay my vows unto Jehovah,<br>Yea, in the presence of all his people.<br>
<sup>15</sup> Precious in the sight of Jehovah<br>Is the death of his saints.<br>
<sup>16</sup> O Jehovah, truly I am thy servant:<br>I am thy servant, the son of thy handmaid;<br>Thou hast loosed my bonds.<br>
<sup>17</sup> I will offer to thee the sacrifice of thanksgiving,<br>And will call upon the name of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>18</sup> I will pay my vows unto Jehovah,<br>Yea, in the presence of all his people,<br>
<sup>19</sup> In the courts of Jehovah’s house,<br>In the midst of thee, O Jerusalem.<sup>e</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _found me_
> - <sup>b</sup> 9. Hebrew _lands_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 10. Or, _believed, when I spake_ thus
> - <sup>d</sup> 11. Or, _alarm_
> - <sup>e</sup> 19. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 117

### A psalm of praise.

<sup>1</sup> O praise Jehovah, all ye nations;<br>Laud him, all ye peoples.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For his lovingkindness is great toward us;<br>And the truth of Jehovah _endureth_ for ever.<sup>a</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 118

### Thanksgiving for Jehovah’s saving goodness.

<sup>1</sup> Oh give thanks unto Jehovah; for he is good;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel now say,<br>That his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let the house of Aaron now say,<br>That his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Let them now that fear Jehovah say,<br>That his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Out of my distress I called upon <sup>a</sup>Jehovah:<sup>b</sup><br>Jehovah answered me _and set me_ in a large place.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Jehovah is on my side; I will not fear:<br>What can man do unto me?<br>
<sup>7</sup> Jehovah is on my side among them that help me:<br>Therefore shall I see _my desire_ upon them that hate me.<br>
<sup>8</sup> It is better to take refuge in Jehovah<br>Than to put confidence in man.<br>
<sup>9</sup> It is better to take refuge in Jehovah<br>Than to put confidence in princes.<br>
<sup>10</sup> All nations compassed me about:<br>In the name of Jehovah I will cut them off.<br>
<sup>11</sup> They compassed me about; yea, they compassed me about:<br>In the name of Jehovah I will cut them off.<br>
<sup>12</sup> They compassed me about like bees; they are quenched as the fire of thorns:<br>In the name of Jehovah I will cut them off.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Thou didst thrust sore at me that I might fall;<br>But Jehovah helped me.<br>
<sup>14</sup> <sup>c</sup>Jehovah is my strength and song;<br>And he is become my salvation.<br>
<sup>15</sup> The voice of rejoicing and salvation is in the tents of the righteous:<br>The right hand of Jehovah doeth valiantly.<br>
<sup>16</sup> The right hand of Jehovah is exalted:<br>The right hand of Jehovah doeth valiantly.<br>
<sup>17</sup> I shall not die, but live,<br>And declare the works of <sup>d</sup>Jehovah.<br>
<sup>18</sup> <sup>e</sup>Jehovah hath chastened me sore;<br>But he hath not given me over unto death.<br>
<sup>19</sup> Open to me the gates of righteousness:<br>I will enter into them, I will give thanks unto <sup>f</sup>Jehovah.<br>
<sup>20</sup> This is the gate of Jehovah;<br>The righteous shall enter into it.<br>
<sup>21</sup> I will give thanks unto thee; for thou hast answered me,<br>And art become my salvation.<br>
<sup>22</sup> The stone which the builders rejected<br>Is become the head of the corner.<br>
<sup>23</sup> This is <sup>g</sup>Jehovah’s doing;<br>It is marvellous in our eyes.<br>
<sup>24</sup> This is the day which Jehovah hath made;<br>We will rejoice and be glad in it.<br>
<sup>25</sup> Save now, we beseech thee, O Jehovah:<br>O Jehovah, we beseech thee, send now prosperity.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Blessed be he that <sup>h</sup>cometh in the name of Jehovah:<br>We have blessed you out of the house of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>27</sup> Jehovah is God, and he hath given us light:<br>Bind the sacrifice with cords, even unto the horns of the altar.<br>
<sup>28</sup> Thou art my God, and I will give thanks unto thee:<br>Thou art my God, I will exalt thee.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Oh give thanks unto Jehovah; for he is good;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 5. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 14. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 17. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 18. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>f</sup> 19. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>g</sup> 23. Hebrew _from Jehovah_.
> - <sup>h</sup> 26. Or, _entereth_

## Chapter 119

### Meditations and prayers relating to the law of God.


#### א ALEPH.
<sup>1</sup> Blessed are they that are perfect in the way,<br>Who walk in the law of Jehovah.<sup>a</sup><br>
<sup>2</sup> Blessed are they that keep his testimonies,<br>That seek him with the whole heart.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Yea, they do no unrighteousness;<br>They walk in his ways.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Thou hast commanded _us_ thy precepts,<br>That we should observe them diligently.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Oh that my ways were established<br>To observe thy statutes!<br>
<sup>6</sup> Then shall I not be put to shame,<br>When I have respect unto all thy commandments.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I will give thanks unto thee with uprightness of heart,<br>When I learn thy righteous judgments.<br>
<sup>8</sup> I will observe thy statutes:<br>Oh forsake me not utterly.<br>

#### ב BETH.
<sup>9</sup> Wherewith shall a young man cleanse his way?<br>By taking heed _thereto_ according to thy word.<br>
<sup>10</sup> With my whole heart have I sought thee:<br>Oh let me not wander from thy commandments.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Thy word have I laid up in my heart,<br>That I might not sin against thee.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Blessed art thou, O Jehovah:<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>13</sup> With my lips have I declared<br>All the ordinances of thy mouth.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I have rejoiced in the way of thy testimonies,<br>As much as in all riches.<br>
<sup>15</sup> I will meditate on thy precepts,<br>And have respect unto thy ways.<br>
<sup>16</sup> I will delight myself in thy statutes:<br>I will not forget thy word.<br>

#### ג GIMEL.
<sup>17</sup> Deal bountifully with thy servant, that I may live;<br>So will I observe thy word.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Open thou mine eyes, that I may behold<br>Wondrous things out of thy law.<br>
<sup>19</sup> I am a sojourner in the earth:<br>Hide not thy commandments from me.<br>
<sup>20</sup> My soul breaketh for the longing<br>That it hath unto thine ordinances at all times.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Thou hast rebuked the proud <sup>b</sup>that are cursed,<br>That do wander from thy commandments.<br>
<sup>22</sup> Take away from me reproach and contempt;<br>For I have kept thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Princes also sat and talked against me;<br>_But_ thy servant did meditate on thy statutes.<br>
<sup>24</sup> Thy testimonies also are my delight<br>_And_<sup>c</sup> my counsellors.<br>

#### ד DALETH.
<sup>25</sup> My soul cleaveth unto the dust:<br>Quicken thou me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>26</sup> I declared my ways, and thou answeredst me:<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>27</sup> Make me to understand the way of thy precepts:<br>So shall I meditate on thy wondrous works.<br>
<sup>28</sup> My soul <sup>d</sup>melteth for heaviness:<br>Strengthen thou me according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>29</sup> Remove from me the way of falsehood;<br>And grant me thy law graciously.<br>
<sup>30</sup> I have chosen the way of faithfulness:<br>Thine ordinances have I set _before me_.<br>
<sup>31</sup> I cleave unto thy testimonies:<br>O Jehovah, put me not to shame.<br>
<sup>32</sup> I will run the way of thy commandments,<br>When thou shalt enlarge my heart.<br>

#### ה HE.
<sup>33</sup> Teach me, O Jehovah, the way of thy statutes;<br>And I shall keep it unto the end.<br>
<sup>34</sup> Give me understanding, and I shall keep thy law;<br>Yea, I shall observe it with my whole heart.<br>
<sup>35</sup> Make me to go in the path of thy commandments;<br>For therein do I delight.<br>
<sup>36</sup> Incline my heart unto thy testimonies,<br>And not to covetousness.<br>
<sup>37</sup> Turn away mine eyes from beholding vanity,<br>And quicken me in thy ways.<br>
<sup>38</sup> Confirm unto thy servant thy word,<sup>e</sup><br>Which _is in order_ unto the fear of thee.<br>
<sup>39</sup> Turn away my reproach whereof I am afraid;<br>For thine ordinances are good.<br>
<sup>40</sup> Behold, I have longed after thy precepts:<br>Quicken me in thy righteousness.<br>

#### ו VAV.
<sup>41</sup> Let thy lovingkindnesses also come unto me, O Jehovah,<br>Even thy salvation, according to thy word.<br>
<sup>42</sup> So shall I have an answer for him that reproacheth me;<br>For I trust in thy word.<br>
<sup>43</sup> And take not the word of truth utterly out of my mouth;<br>For I have hoped in thine ordinances.<br>
<sup>44</sup> So shall I observe thy law continually<br>For ever and ever.<br>
<sup>45</sup> And I shall walk at liberty;<br>For I have sought thy precepts.<br>
<sup>46</sup> I will also speak of thy testimonies before kings,<br>And shall not be put to shame.<br>
<sup>47</sup> And I will delight myself in thy commandments,<br>Which I have loved.<br>
<sup>48</sup> I will lift up my hands also unto thy commandments, which I have loved;<br>And I will meditate on thy statutes.<br>

#### ז ZAYIN.
<sup>49</sup> Remember the word unto thy servant,<br>Because <sup>f</sup>thou hast made me to hope.<br>
<sup>50</sup> This is my comfort in my affliction;<sup>g</sup><br>For thy word hath quickened me.<br>
<sup>51</sup> The proud have had me greatly in derision:<br>_Yet_ have I not swerved from thy law.<br>
<sup>52</sup> I have remembered thine ordinances of old, O Jehovah,<br>And have comforted myself.<br>
<sup>53</sup> <sup>h</sup>Hot indignation hath taken hold upon me,<br>Because of the wicked that forsake thy law.<br>
<sup>54</sup> Thy statutes have been my songs<br>In the house of my pilgrimage.<br>
<sup>55</sup> I have remembered thy name, O Jehovah, in the night,<br>And have observed thy law.<br>
<sup>56</sup> This I have had,<sup>i</sup><br>Because I have kept thy precepts.<br>

#### ח HHETH.
<sup>57</sup> Jehovah <sup>j</sup>is my portion:<br>I have said that I would observe thy words.<br>
<sup>58</sup> I entreated thy favor with my whole heart:<br>Be merciful unto me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>59</sup> I thought on my ways,<br>And turned my feet unto thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>60</sup> I made haste, and delayed not,<br>To observe thy commandments.<br>
<sup>61</sup> The cords of the wicked have wrapped me round;<br>_But_ I have not forgotten thy law.<br>
<sup>62</sup> At midnight I will rise to give thanks unto thee<br>Because of thy righteous ordinances.<br>
<sup>63</sup> I am a companion of all them that fear thee,<br>And of them that observe thy precepts.<br>
<sup>64</sup> The earth, O Jehovah, is full of thy lovingkindness:<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>

#### ט TETH.
<sup>65</sup> Thou hast dealt well with thy servant,<br>O Jehovah, according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>66</sup> Teach me good judgment and knowledge;<br>For I have believed in thy commandments.<br>
<sup>67</sup> Before I was afflicted I went astray;<br>But now I observe thy word.<br>
<sup>68</sup> Thou art good, and doest good;<br>Teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>69</sup> The proud have forged a lie against me:<br>With my whole heart will I keep thy precepts.<br>
<sup>70</sup> Their heart is as fat as grease;<br>But I delight in thy law.<br>
<sup>71</sup> It is good for me that I have been afflicted;<br>That I may learn thy statutes.<br>
<sup>72</sup> The law of thy mouth is better unto me<br>Than thousands of gold and silver.<br>

#### י YODH.
<sup>73</sup> Thy hands have made me and fashio<sup>k</sup>ned me:<br>Give me understanding, that I may learn thy commandments.<br>
<sup>74</sup> They that fear thee shall see me and be glad,<br>Because I have hoped in thy word.<br>
<sup>75</sup> I know, O Jehovah, that thy judgments are righteous,<br>And that in faithfulness thou hast afflicted me.<br>
<sup>76</sup> Let, I pray thee, thy lovingkindness be for my comfort,<br>According to thy word unto thy servant.<br>
<sup>77</sup> Let thy tender mercies come unto me, that I may live;<br>For thy law is my delight.<br>
<sup>78</sup> Let the proud be put to shame; for they have overthrown me <sup>l</sup>wrongfully:<br>_But_ I will meditate on thy precepts.<br>
<sup>79</sup> Let those that fear thee turn unto me;<sup>m</sup><br>And they shall know thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>80</sup> Let my heart be perfect in thy statutes,<br>That I be not put to shame.<br>

#### כ KAPH.
<sup>81</sup> My soul fainteth for thy salvation;<br>_But_ I hope in thy word.<br>
<sup>82</sup> Mine eyes fail for thy word,<br>While I say, When wilt thou comfort me?<br>
<sup>83</sup> For I am become like a wine-skin in the smoke;<br>Yet do I not forget thy statutes.<br>
<sup>84</sup> How many are the days of thy servant?<br>When wilt thou execute judgment on them that persecute me?<br>
<sup>85</sup> The proud have digged pits for me,<br>Who are not according to thy law.<br>
<sup>86</sup> All thy commandments are faithful:<br>They persecute me <sup>n</sup>wrongfully; help thou me.<br>
<sup>87</sup> They had almost consumed me upon earth;<br>But I forsook not thy precepts.<br>
<sup>88</sup> Quicken me after thy lovingkindness;<br>So shall I observe the testimony of thy mouth.<br>

#### ל LAMEDH.
<sup>89</sup> For ever, O Jehovah,<br>Thy word is settled in heaven.<br>
<sup>90</sup> Thy faithfulness is unto all generations:<br>Thou hast established the earth, and it abideth.<br>
<sup>91</sup> <sup>o</sup>They abide this day according to thine ordinances;<br>For all things are thy servants.<br>
<sup>92</sup> Unless thy law had been my delight,<br>I should then have perished in mine affliction.<br>
<sup>93</sup> I will never forget thy precepts;<br>For with them thou hast quickened me.<br>
<sup>94</sup> I am thine, save me;<br>For I have sought thy precepts.<br>
<sup>95</sup> The wicked have waited for me, to destroy me;<br>_But_ I will consider thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>96</sup> I have seen an end of all perfection;<br>_But_ thy commandment is exceeding broad.<br>

#### מ MEM.
<sup>97</sup> Oh how love I thy law!<br>It is my meditation all the day.<br>
<sup>98</sup> <sup>p</sup>Thy commandments make me wiser than mine enemies;<br>For they are ever with me.<br>
<sup>99</sup> I have more understanding than all my teachers;<br>For thy testimonies are my meditation.<br>
<sup>100</sup> I understand more than the aged,<br>Because I have kept thy precepts.<br>
<sup>101</sup> I have refrained my feet from every evil way,<br>That I might observe thy word.<br>
<sup>102</sup> I have not turned aside from thine ordinances;<br>For thou hast taught me.<br>
<sup>103</sup> How sweet are thy words unto my <sup>q</sup>taste!<br>_Yea, sweeter_ than honey to my mouth!<br>
<sup>104</sup> Through thy precepts I get understanding:<br>Therefore I hate every false way.<br>

#### נ NUN.
<sup>105</sup> Thy word is a lamp unto my feet,<br>And light unto my path.<br>
<sup>106</sup> I have sworn, and have confirmed it,<br>That I will observe thy righteous ordinances.<br>
<sup>107</sup> I am afflicted very much:<br>Quicken me, O Jehovah, according unto thy word.<br>
<sup>108</sup> Accept, I beseech thee, the freewill-offerings of my mouth, O Jehovah,<br>And teach me thine ordinances.<br>
<sup>109</sup> My soul is continually in my hand;<br>Yet do I not forget thy law.<br>
<sup>110</sup> The wicked have laid a snare for me;<br>Yet have I not gone astray from thy precepts.<br>
<sup>111</sup> Thy testimonies have I taken as a heritage for ever;<br>For they are the rejoicing of my heart.<br>
<sup>112</sup> I have inclined my heart to perform thy statutes<br>For ever, even unto the end.<br>

#### ס SAMEKH.
<sup>113</sup> I hate them that are of a double mind;<br>But thy law do I love.<br>
<sup>114</sup> Thou art my hiding-place and my shield:<br>I hope in thy word.<br>
<sup>115</sup> Depart from me, ye evil-doers,<br>That I may keep the commandments of my God.<br>
<sup>116</sup> Uphold me according unto thy word, that I may live;<br>And let me not be ashamed of my hope.<br>
<sup>117</sup> Hold thou me up, and I shall be safe,<br>And shall have respect unto thy statutes continually.<br>
<sup>118</sup> Thou hast set at nought all them that err from thy statutes;<br>For their deceit is <sup>r</sup>falsehood.<br>
<sup>119</sup> Thou <sup>s</sup>puttest away all the wicked of the earth like dross:<br>Therefore I love thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>120</sup> My flesh trembleth for fear of thee;<br>And I am afraid of thy judgments.<br>

#### ע AYIN.
<sup>121</sup> I have done justice and righteousness:<br>Leave me not to mine oppressors.<br>
<sup>122</sup> Be surety for thy servant for good:<br>Let not the proud oppress me.<br>
<sup>123</sup> Mine eyes fail for thy salvation,<br>And for thy righteous word.<br>
<sup>124</sup> Deal with thy servant according unto thy lovingkindness,<br>And teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>125</sup> I am thy servant; give me understanding,<br>That I may know thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>126</sup> It is time for Jehovah to work;<br>_For_ they have made void thy law.<br>
<sup>127</sup> Therefore I love thy commandments<br>Above gold, yea, above fine gold.<br>
<sup>128</sup> Therefore I esteem all _thy_ precepts concerning all _things_ to be right;<br>_And_ I hate every false way.<br>

#### פ PE.
<sup>129</sup> Thy testimonies are wonderful;<br>Therefore doth my soul keep them.<br>
<sup>130</sup> The opening of thy words giveth light;<br>It giveth understanding unto the simple.<br>
<sup>131</sup> I opened wide my mouth, and panted;<br>For I longed for thy commandments.<br>
<sup>132</sup> Turn thee unto me, and have mercy upon me,<br>As thou usest to do unto those that love thy name.<br>
<sup>133</sup> Establish my footsteps in thy word;<br>And let not any iniquity have dominion over me.<br>
<sup>134</sup> Redeem me from the oppression of man:<br>So will I observe thy precepts.<br>
<sup>135</sup> Make thy face to shine upon thy servant;<br>And teach me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>136</sup> Streams of water run down mine eyes,<br>Because they observe not thy law.<br>

#### צ TSADHE.
<sup>137</sup> Righteous art thou, O Jehovah,<br>And upright are thy ju<sup>t</sup>dgments.<br>
<sup>138</sup> Thou hast commanded thy testimonies in righteousness<br>And very faithfulness.<br>
<sup>139</sup> My zeal hath <sup>u</sup>consumed me,<br>Because mine adversaries have forgotten thy words.<br>
<sup>140</sup> Thy word is very <sup>v</sup>pure;<br>Therefore thy servant loveth it.<br>
<sup>141</sup> I am small and despised;<br>_Yet_ do I not forget thy precepts.<br>
<sup>142</sup> Thy righteousness is an everlasting righteousness,<br>And thy law is truth.<br>
<sup>143</sup> Trouble and anguish have <sup>w</sup>taken hold on me;<br>_Yet_ thy commandments are my delight.<br>
<sup>144</sup> Thy testimonies are righteous for ever:<br>Give me understanding, and I shall live.<br>

#### ק QOPH.
<sup>145</sup> I have called with my whole heart; answer me, O Jehovah:<br>I will keep thy statutes.<br>
<sup>146</sup> I have called unto thee; save me,<br>And I shall observe thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>147</sup> I anticipated the dawning of the morning, and cried:<br>I hoped in thy words.<br>
<sup>148</sup> Mine eyes anticipated the night-watches,<br>That I might meditate on thy word.<br>
<sup>149</sup> Hear my voice according unto thy lovingkindness:<br>Quicken me, O Jehovah, <sup>x</sup>according to thine ordinances.<br>
<sup>150</sup> They draw nigh <sup>y</sup>that follow after wickedness;<br>They are far from thy law.<br>
<sup>151</sup> Thou art nigh, O Jehovah;<br>And all thy commandments are truth.<br>
<sup>152</sup> Of old have I known from thy testimonies,<br>That thou hast founded them for ever.<br>

#### ר RESH.
<sup>153</sup> Consider mine affliction, and deliver me;<br>For I do not forget thy law.<br>
<sup>154</sup> Plead thou my cause, and redeem me:<br>Quicken me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>155</sup> Salvation is far from the wicked;<br>For they seek not thy statutes.<br>
<sup>156</sup> Great are thy tender mercies, O Jehovah:<br>Quicken me according to thine ordinances.<br>
<sup>157</sup> Many are my persecutors and mine adversaries;<br>_Yet_ have I not swerved from thy testimonies.<br>
<sup>158</sup> I beheld the treacherous, and <sup>z</sup>was grieved,<br>Because they observe not thy word.<br>
<sup>159</sup> Consider how I love thy precepts:<br>Quicken me, O Jehovah, according to thy lovingkindness.<br>
<sup>160</sup> The sum of thy word is truth;<br>And every one of thy righteous ordinances _endureth_ for ever.<br>

#### ש SHIN.
<sup>161</sup> Princes have persecuted me without a cause;<br>But my heart standeth in awe of thy words.<br>
<sup>162</sup> I rejoice at thy word,<br>As one that findeth great spoil.<br>
<sup>163</sup> I hate and abhor falsehood;<br>_But_ thy law do I love.<br>
<sup>164</sup> Seven times a day do I praise thee,<br>Because of thy righteous ordinances.<br>
<sup>165</sup> Great peace have they that love thy law;<br>And they have no occasion of stumbling.<br>
<sup>166</sup> I have hoped for thy salvation, O Jehovah,<br>And have done thy commandments.<br>
<sup>167</sup> My soul hath observed thy testimonies;<br>And I love them exceedingly.<br>
<sup>168</sup> I have observed thy precepts and thy testimonies;<br>For all my ways are before thee.<br>

#### ת TAV.
<sup>169</sup> Let my cry come near before thee, O Jehovah:<br>Give me understanding according to thy word.<br>
<sup>170</sup> Let my supplication come before thee:<br>Deliver me according to thy word.<br>
<sup>171</sup> Let my lips utter praise;<br>For thou teachest me thy statutes.<br>
<sup>172</sup> Let my tongue sing of thy word;<br>For all thy commandments are righteousness.<br>
<sup>173</sup> Let thy hand be ready to help me;<br>For I have chosen thy precepts.<br>
<sup>174</sup> I have longed for thy salvation, O Jehovah;<br>And thy law is my delight.<br>
<sup>175</sup> Let my soul live, and it shall praise thee;<br>And let thine ordinances help me.<br>
<sup>176</sup> I have gone astray like a lost sheep; seek thy servant;<br>For I do not forget thy commandments.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _upright in way_
> - <sup>b</sup> 21. Or, _Cursed are they that etc._
> - <sup>c</sup> 24. Hebrew _the men of my counsel_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 28. Hebrew _droppeth_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 38. Or, _Who_ is devoted _to_
> - <sup>f</sup> 49. Or, _Wherein_
> - <sup>g</sup> 50. Or, _That_
> - <sup>h</sup> 53. Or, _Horror_
> - <sup>i</sup> 56. Or, _That_
> - <sup>j</sup> 57. Or, _Jehovah is my portion, have I said: That I may observe etc._
> - <sup>k</sup> 73. Or, _established_
> - <sup>l</sup> 78. Or, _with falsehood_
> - <sup>m</sup> 79. Another reading is, _Even they that know_.
> - <sup>n</sup> 86. Or, _with falsehood_
> - <sup>o</sup> 91. Or, _As for thine ordinances, they abide this day_
> - <sup>p</sup> 98. Or, _Thou through thy commandments makest_
> - <sup>q</sup> 103. Hebrew _palate_.
> - <sup>r</sup> 118. Or, _vain_
> - <sup>s</sup> 119. Hebrew _causest to cease_.
> - <sup>t</sup> 137. Or, _in thy judgments_
> - <sup>u</sup> 139. Hebrew _cut me off_.
> - <sup>v</sup> 140. Hebrew _tried_, or, _refined_.
> - <sup>w</sup> 143. Or, _found me_
> - <sup>x</sup> 149. Or, _as thou art wont_
> - <sup>y</sup> 150. Or, _that persecute_ me _with wickedness_
> - <sup>z</sup> 158. Or, _loathed_ them

## Chapter 120

### Prayer for deliverance from the treacherous.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._In my distress I cried unto Jehovah,<br>And he answered me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Deliver my soul, O Jehovah, from lying lips,<br>_And_ from a deceitful tongue.<br>
<sup>3</sup> What shall be given unto thee, and what shall be done more unto thee,<br>Thou deceitful tongue?<br>
<sup>4</sup> <sup>a</sup>Sharp arrows of the mighty,<br>With coals of <sup>b</sup>juniper.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Woe is me, that I sojourn in Meshech,<br>That I dwell among the tents of Kedar!<br>
<sup>6</sup> My soul hath long had her dwelling<br>With him that hateth peace.<br>
<sup>7</sup> I am _for_ peace:<br>But when I speak, they are for war.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, It is as _the sharp arrows of the mighty_ man
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _broom_

## Chapter 121

### Jehovah the keeper of his people.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._I will lift up mine eyes unto the mountains:<br>From whence shall my help come?<br>
<sup>2</sup> My help _cometh_ from Jehovah,<br>Who made heaven and earth.<br>
<sup>3</sup> <sup>a</sup>He will not suffer thy foot to be moved:<br>He that keepeth thee will not slumber.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Behold, he that keepeth Israel<br>Will neither slumber nor sleep.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Jehovah is thy keeper:<br>Jehovah is thy shade upon thy right hand.<br>
<sup>6</sup> The sun shall not smite thee by day,<br>Nor the moon by night.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Jehovah will keep thee from all evil;<br>He will keep thy soul.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Jehovah will keep thy going out and thy coming in<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _Let him not suffer . . . Let him not slumber that etc._

## Chapter 122

### Prayer for the peace of Jerusalem.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents; of David._I was glad when they said unto me,<br>Let us go unto the house of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Our feet <sup>a</sup>are standing<br>Within thy gates, O Jerusalem,<br>
<sup>3</sup> Jerusalem, that art builded<br>As a city that is compact together;<br>
<sup>4</sup> Whither the tribes go up, even the tribes of <sup>b</sup>Jehovah,<br>_For_<sup>c</sup> an ordinance for Israel,<br>To give thanks unto the name of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For there <sup>d</sup>are set thrones for judgment,<br>The thrones of the house of David.<br>
<sup>6</sup> <sup>e</sup>Pray for the peace of Jerusalem:<sup>f</sup><br>They shall prosper that love thee.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Peace be within thy walls,<br>And prosperity within thy palaces.<br>
<sup>8</sup> For my brethren and companions’ sakes,<br>I will now <sup>g</sup>say, Peace be within thee.<br>
<sup>9</sup> For the sake of the house of Jehovah our God<br>I will seek thy good.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _have stood_
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 4. Hebrew _a testimony_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 5. Or, _were_
> - <sup>e</sup> 6. Or, _Salute ye Jerusalem_
> - <sup>f</sup> 6. Or, _May they_
> - <sup>g</sup> 8. Or, _speak peace concerning thee_

## Chapter 123

### An expectant prayer for Jehovah’s help.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Unto thee do I lift up mine eyes,<br>O thou that sittest in the heavens.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Behold, as the eyes of servants _look_ unto the hand of their master,<br>As the eyes of a maid unto the hand of her mistress;<br>So our eyes _look_ unto Jehovah our God,<br>Until he have mercy upon us.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Have mercy upon us, O Jehovah, have mercy upon us;<br>For we are exceedingly filled with contempt.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Our soul is exceedingly filled<br>With the scoffing of those that are at ease,<br>And with the contempt of the proud.<br>

## Chapter 124

### Praise for rescue from enemies.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents; of David._If it had not been Jehovah who was on our side,<br>Let Israel now say,<br>
<sup>2</sup> If it had not been Jehovah who was on our side,<br>When men rose up against us;<br>
<sup>3</sup> Then they had swallowed us up alive,<br>When their wrath was kindled against us:<br>
<sup>4</sup> Then the waters had overwhelmed us,<br>The stream had gone over our soul;<br>
<sup>5</sup> Then the proud waters had gone over our soul.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Blessed be Jehovah,<br>Who hath not given us as a prey to their teeth.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Our soul is escaped as a bird out of the snare of the fowlers:<br>The snare is broken, and we are escaped.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Our help is in the name of Jehovah,<br>Who made heaven and earth.<br>

## Chapter 125

### Jehovah round about his people.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._They that trust in Jehovah<br>Are as mount Zion, which cannot be moved, but abideth for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> As the mountains are round about Jerusalem,<br>So Jehovah is round about his people<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For the sceptre of wickedness shall not rest upon the lot of the righteous;<br>That the righteous put not forth their hands unto iniquity.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Do good, O Jehovah, unto those that are good,<br>And to them that are upright in their hearts.<br>
<sup>5</sup> But as for such as turn aside unto their crooked ways,<br>Jehovah will lead them forth with the workers of iniquity.<br>Peace be upon Israel.<br>

## Chapter 126

### Thanksgiving for return from captivity.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._When Jehovah brought back those that returned to Zion,<br>We were like unto them that dream.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Then was our mouth filled with laughter,<br>And our tongue with singing:<br>Then said they among the nations,<br>Jehovah hath done great things for them.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Jehovah hath done great things for us,<br>_Whereof_ we are glad.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Turn again our captivity, O Jehovah,<br>As the streams in the South.<br>
<sup>5</sup> They that sow in tears shall reap in joy.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He that goeth forth and weepeth, <sup>a</sup>bearing seed for sowing,<br>Shall doubtless come again with joy, bringing his sheaves _with him_.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Or, _bearing the measure of seed_

## Chapter 127

### Prosperity comes from Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents; of Solomon._Except Jehovah build the house,<br>They labor in vain that build it:<br>Except Jehovah keep the city,<br>The watchman waketh but in vain.<br>
<sup>2</sup> It is vain for you to rise up early,<br>To take rest late,<br>To eat the bread of toil;<br>_For_ so he giveth unto his beloved <sup>a</sup>sleep.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Lo, children are a heritage of Jehovah;<br>_And_ the fruit of the womb is _his_ reward.<br>
<sup>4</sup> As arrows in the hand of a mighty man,<br>So are the children of youth.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Happy is the man that hath his quiver full of them:<br>They shall not be put to shame,<br>When they speak with their enemies in the gate.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _in sleep_

## Chapter 128

### Blessedness of the fear of Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Blessed is every one that feareth Jehovah,<br>That walketh in his ways.<br>
<sup>2</sup> For thou shalt eat the labor of thy hands:<br>Happy shalt thou be, and it shall be well with thee.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thy wife shall be as a fruitful vine,<br>In the innermost parts of thy house;<br>Thy children like olive plants,<br>Round about thy table.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Behold, thus shall the man be blessed<br>That feareth Jehovah.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Jehovah bless thee out of Zion:<br>And see thou the good of Jerusalem all the days of thy life.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Yea, see thou thy children’s children.<sup>a</sup><br>Peace be upon Israel.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 6. Or, And _peace upon Israel_

## Chapter 129

### Prayer for the overthrow of Zion’s enemies.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Many a time have they afflicted me from my youth up,<br>Let Israel n<sup>a</sup>ow say,<br>
<sup>2</sup> <sup>b</sup>Many a time have they afflicted me from my youth up:<br>Yet they have not prevailed against me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> The plowers plowed upon my back;<br>They made long their furrows.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Jehovah is righteous:<br>He hath cut asunder the cords of the wicked.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let them be put to shame and turned backward,<br>All they that hate Zion.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let them be as the grass upon the housetops,<br>Which withereth before it <sup>c</sup>groweth up;<br>
<sup>7</sup> Wherewith the reaper filleth not his hand,<br>Nor he that bindeth sheaves, his bosom:<br>
<sup>8</sup> Neither do they that go by say,<br>The blessing of Jehovah be upon you;<br>We bless you in the name of Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Or, _Much_
> - <sup>b</sup> 2. Or, _Much_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Or, _is plucked up_

## Chapter 130

### Hope in the Lord’s forgiving love.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Out of the depths have I cried unto thee, O Jehovah.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Lord, hear my voice:<br>Let thine ears be attentive<br>To the voice of my supplications.<br>
<sup>3</sup> If thou, <sup>a</sup>Jehovah, shouldest mark iniquities,<br>O Lord, who could stand?<br>
<sup>4</sup> But there is forgiveness with thee,<br>That thou mayest be feared.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I wait for Jehovah, my soul doth wait,<br>And in his word do I hope.<br>
<sup>6</sup> My soul _waiteth_ for the Lord<br>More than watchmen _wait_ for the morning;<br>_Yea, more than_ watchmen for the morning.<br>
<sup>7</sup> O Israel, hope in Jehovah;<br>For with Jehovah there is lovingkindness,<br>And with him is plenteous redemption.<br>
<sup>8</sup> And he will redeem Israel<br>From all his iniquities.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Hebrew _Jah_.

## Chapter 131

### Childlike trust in Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents; of David._Jehovah, my heart is not haughty, nor mine eyes lofty;<br>Neither do I exercise myself in great matters,<br>Or in things too wonde<sup>a</sup>rful for me.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Surely I have stilled and quieted my soul;<br>Like a weaned child with his mother,<br>Like a weaned child is my soul within me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> O Israel, hope in Jehovah<br>From this time forth and for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _walk_.

## Chapter 132

### Prayer for Jehovah’s blessing upon the sanctuary.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Jehovah, remember for David<br>All his affliction;<br>
<sup>2</sup> How he sware unto Jehovah,<br>And vowed unto the Mighty One of Jacob:<br>
<sup>3</sup> Surely I will not come into the <sup>a</sup>tabernacle of my house,<br>Nor go up into <sup>b</sup>my bed;<br>
<sup>4</sup> I will not give sleep to mine eyes,<br>Or slumber to mine eyelids;<br>
<sup>5</sup> Until I find out a place for Jehovah,<sup>c</sup><br>A tabernacle for the Mighty One of Jacob.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Lo, we heard of it in <sup>d</sup>Ephrathah:<br>We found it in the field of <sup>e</sup>the wood.<br>
<sup>7</sup> We will go into his tabernacles;<br>We will worship at his footstool.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Arise, O Jehovah, into thy resting-place;<br>Thou, and the ark of thy strength.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Let thy priests be clothed with righteousness;<br>And let thy saints shout for joy.<br>
<sup>10</sup> For thy servant David’s sake<br>Turn not away the face of thine anointed.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Jehovah hath sworn unto David in truth;<br>He will not turn from it:<br>Of the fruit of thy body will I set upon thy throne.<br>
<sup>12</sup> If thy children will keep my covenant<br>And my testimony that I shall teach them,<br>Their children also shall sit upon thy throne for evermore.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For Jehovah hath chosen Zion;<br>He hath desired it for his habitation.<br>
<sup>14</sup> This is my resting-place for ever:<br>Here will I dwell; for I have desired it.<br>
<sup>15</sup> I will <sup>f</sup>abundantly bless her provision:<br>I will satisfy her poor with bread.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Her priests also will I clothe with salvation;<br>And her saints shall shout aloud for joy.<br>
<sup>17</sup> There will I make <sup>g</sup>the horn of David to bud:<br>I have <sup>h</sup>ordained a lamp for mine anointed.<br>
<sup>18</sup> His enemies will I clothe with shame;<br>But upon himself shall his crown flourish.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Hebrew _tent_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Hebrew _the couch of my bed_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Hebrew _Tabernacles_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 6. Or, _Ephraim_
> - <sup>e</sup> 6. Or, _Jaar_. See 1 Chr. 13:5.
> - <sup>f</sup> 15. Or, _surely_
> - <sup>g</sup> 17. Or, _a horn to spring forth unto David_
> - <sup>h</sup> 17. Or, _prepared_

## Chapter 133

### The excellency of brotherly unity.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents; of David._Behold, how good and how pleasant it is<br>For brethren to dwell together in unity!<br>
<sup>2</sup> It is like the precious oil upon the head,<br>That ran down upon the beard,<br>Even Aaron’s beard;<br>That came down upon the <sup>a</sup>skirt of his garments;<br>
<sup>3</sup> Like the dew of Hermon,<br>That cometh down upon the mountains of Zion:<br>For there Jehovah commanded the blessing,<br>Even life for evermore.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _collar_

## Chapter 134

### Greetings of night-watchers.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Song of Ascents._Behold, bless ye Jehovah, all ye servants of Jehovah,<br>That by night stand in the house of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Lift up your hands <sup>a</sup>to the sanctuary,<br>And bless ye Jehovah.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Jehovah bless thee out of Zion;<br>Even he that made heaven and earth.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _in holiness_

## Chapter 135

### Praise for Jehovah’s wonderful works. Vanity of idols.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Praise ye the name of Jehovah;<br>Praise <sup>a</sup>_him_, O ye servants of Jehovah,<br>
<sup>2</sup> Ye that stand in the house of Jehovah,<br>In the courts of the house of our God.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise ye Jehovah; for Jehovah is good:<br>Sing praises unto his name; for it is pleasant.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For <sup>b</sup>Jehovah hath chosen Jacob unto himself,<br>_And_ Israel for his own possession.<br>
<sup>5</sup> For I know that Jehovah is great,<br>And that our Lord is above all gods.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Whatsoever Jehovah pleased, that hath he done,<br>In heaven and in earth, in the seas and in all deeps;<br>
<sup>7</sup> Who causeth the vapors to ascend from the ends of the earth;<br>Who maketh lightnings for the rain;<br>Who bringeth forth the wind out of his treasuries;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who smote the first-born of Egypt,<br>Both of man and beast;<br>
<sup>9</sup> Who sent signs and wonders into the midst of thee, O Egypt,<br>Upon Pharaoh, and upon all his servants;<br>
<sup>10</sup> Who smote many <sup>c</sup>nations,<br>And slew mighty kings,<br>
<sup>11</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites,<br>And Og king of Bashan,<br>And all the kingdoms of Canaan,<br>
<sup>12</sup> And gave their land for a heritage,<br>A heritage unto Israel his people.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Thy name, O Jehovah, _endureth_ for ever;<br>Thy memorial _name_, O Jehovah, throughout all generations.<br>
<sup>14</sup> For Jehovah will judge his people,<br>And repent himself concerning his servants.<br>
<sup>15</sup> The idols of the nations are silver and gold,<br>The work of men’s hands.<br>
<sup>16</sup> They have mouths, but they speak not;<br>Eyes have they, but they see not;<br>
<sup>17</sup> They have ears, but they hear not;<br>Neither is there any breath in their mouths.<br>
<sup>18</sup> They that make them shall be like unto them;<br>Yea, every one that trusteth in them.<br>
<sup>19</sup> O house of Israel, bless ye Jehovah:<br>O house of Aaron, bless ye Jehovah:<br>
<sup>20</sup> O house of Levi, bless ye Jehovah:<br>Ye that fear Jehovah, bless ye Jehovah.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Blessed be Jehovah out of Zion,<br>Who dwelleth at Jerusalem.<sup>d</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 10. Or, _great_
> - <sup>d</sup> 21. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 136

### Thanks for Jehovah’s goodness to Israel.

<sup>1</sup> Oh give thanks unto Jehovah; for he is good;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Oh give thanks unto the God of gods;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Oh give thanks unto the Lord of lords;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>4</sup> To him who alone doeth great wonders;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>5</sup> To him that by understanding made the heavens;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>6</sup> To him that spread forth the earth above the waters;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>7</sup> To him that made great lights;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>8</sup> The sun to rule by day;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>9</sup> The moon and stars to rule by night;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>10</sup> To him that smote Egypt in their first-born;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>11</sup> And brought out Israel from among them;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>12</sup> With a strong hand, and with an outstretched arm;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>13</sup> To him that divided the Red Sea in sunder;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>14</sup> And made Israel to pass through the midst of it;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>15</sup> But <sup>a</sup>overthrew Pharaoh and his host in the Red Sea;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>16</sup> To him that led his people through the wilderness;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>17</sup> To him that smote great kings;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>18</sup> And slew famous kings;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>19</sup> Sihon king of the Amorites;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ forever;<br>
<sup>20</sup> And Og king of Bashan;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>21</sup> And gave their land for a heritage;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>22</sup> Even a heritage unto Israel his servant;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>23</sup> Who remembered us in our low estate;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever;<br>
<sup>24</sup> And hath delivered us from our adversaries;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever:<br>
<sup>25</sup> Who giveth food to all flesh;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>
<sup>26</sup> Oh give thanks unto the God of heaven;<br>For his lovingkindness _endureth_ for ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 15. Hebrew _shook off_.

## Chapter 137

### Sorrows of the exiles in Babylon.

<sup>1</sup> By the rivers of Babylon,<br>There we sat down, yea, we wept,<br>When we remembered Zion.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Upon the willows in the midst thereof<br>We hanged up our harps.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For there they that led us captive required of us <sup>a</sup>songs,<br>And <sup>b</sup>they that wasted us _required of us_ mirth, _saying_,<br>Sing us one of the songs of Zion.<br>
<sup>4</sup> How shall we sing Jehovah’s song<br>In a foreign land?<br>
<sup>5</sup> If I forget thee, O Jerusalem,<br>Let my right hand forget _her skill_.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let my tongue cleave to the roof of my mouth,<br>If I remember thee not;<br>If I prefer not Jerusalem<br>Above my chief joy.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Remember, O Jehovah, against the children of Edom<br>The day of Jerusalem;<br>Who said, Rase it, rase it,<br>Even to the foundation thereof.<br>
<sup>8</sup> O daughter of Babylon, <sup>c</sup>that art to be destroyed,<br>Happy shall he be, that rewardeth thee<br>As thou hast served us.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Happy shall he be, that taketh and dasheth thy little ones<br>Against the rock.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Hebrew _words of song_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 3. Or, _our tormentors_
> - <sup>c</sup> 8. Or, _that art laid waste_

## Chapter 138

### Thanksgiving for Jehovah’s favor.

<sup>1</sup> > __A Psalm_ of David._I will give thee thanks with my whole heart:<br>Before the gods will I sing praises unto thee.<br>
<sup>2</sup> I will worship toward thy holy temple,<br>And give thanks unto thy name for thy lovingkindness and for thy truth:<br>For thou hast magnified thy word above all thy name.<br>
<sup>3</sup> In the day that I called thou answeredst me,<br>Thou didst encourage me with strength in my soul.<br>
<sup>4</sup> All the kings of the earth shall give thee thanks, O Jehovah,<br>For they have heard the words of thy mouth.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Yea, they shall sing of the ways of Jehovah;<br>For great is the glory of Jehovah.<br>
<sup>6</sup> For though Jehovah is high, yet hath he respect unto the lowly;<br>But the haughty he knoweth from afar.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Though I walk in the midst of trouble, thou wilt revive me;<br>Thou wilt stretch forth thy hand against the wrath of mine enemies,<br>And thy right hand will save me.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Jehovah will perfect that which concerneth me:<br>Thy lovingkindness, O Jehovah, _endureth_ for ever;<br>Forsake not the works of thine own hands.<br>

## Chapter 139

### God’s omnipresence and omniscience.

<sup>1</sup> > _For the Chief Musician. A Psalm of David._O Jehovah, thou hast searched me, and known _me_.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Thou knowest my downsitting and mine uprising;<br>Thou understandest my thought afar off.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Thou <sup>a</sup>searchest out my path and my lying down,<br>And art acquainted with all my ways.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For there is not a word in my tongue,<br>But, lo, O Jehovah, thou knowest it altogether.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Thou hast beset me behind and before,<br>And laid thy hand upon me.<br>
<sup>6</sup> _Such_ knowledge is too wonderful for me;<br>It is high, I cannot attain unto it.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Whither shall I go from thy Spirit?<br>Or whither shall I flee from thy presence?<br>
<sup>8</sup> If I ascend up into heaven, thou art there:<br>If I make my bed in Sheol, behold, thou art there.<br>
<sup>9</sup> If I take the wings of the morning,<br>And dwell in the uttermost parts of the sea;<br>
<sup>10</sup> Even there shall thy hand lead me,<br>And thy right hand shall hold me.<br>
<sup>11</sup> If I say, Surely the darkness shall <sup>b</sup>overwhelm me,<sup>c</sup><br>And the light about me shall be night;<br>
<sup>12</sup> Even the darkness hideth not from thee,<br>But the night shineth as the day:<br>The darkness and the light are both alike _to thee_.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For thou didst form my <sup>d</sup>inward parts:<br>Thou didst <sup>e</sup>cover me in my mother’s womb.<br>
<sup>14</sup> I will give thanks unto thee; for I am fearfully and wonderfully made:<br>Wonderful are thy works;<br>And that my soul knoweth right well.<br>
