<sup>15</sup> My frame was not hidden from thee,<br>When I was made in secret,<br>_And_ curiously wrought in the lowest parts of the earth.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Thine eyes did see mine unformed substance;<br>And in thy book they were all written,<br>_Even_ the days that were ordained _for me_,<br>When as yet there was none of them.<br>
<sup>17</sup> How precious also are thy thoughts unto me, O God!<br>How great is the sum of them!<br>
<sup>18</sup> If I should count them, they are more in number than the sand:<br>When I awake, I am still with thee.<br>
<sup>19</sup> <sup>f</sup>Surely thou wilt slay the wicked, O God:<br>Depart from me therefore, ye bloodthirsty men.<br>
<sup>20</sup> For they <sup>g</sup>speak against thee wickedly,<br>And thine enemies <sup>h</sup>take _thy name_ in vain.<br>
<sup>21</sup> Do not I hate them, O Jehovah, that hate thee?<br>And <sup>i</sup>am not I grieved with those that rise up against thee?<br>
<sup>22</sup> I hate them with perfect hatred:<br>They are become mine enemies.<br>
<sup>23</sup> Search me, O God, and know my heart:<br>Try me, and know my thoughts;<br>
<sup>24</sup> And see if there be any <sup>j</sup>wicked way in me,<br>And lead me in the way everlasting.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _winnowest_
> - <sup>b</sup> 11. Or, _cover_
> - <sup>c</sup> 11. Or, _Then the night shall be light about me_
> - <sup>d</sup> 13. Hebrew _reins_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 13. Or, _knit me together_
> - <sup>f</sup> 19. Or, _Oh that thou wouldest slay_
> - <sup>g</sup> 20. Or, _utter thy name_ (Hebrew _thee_). Or, as otherwise read, _rebel against thee_
> - <sup>h</sup> 20. Or, _lift themselves up_ against thee _for vanity_
> - <sup>i</sup> 21. Or, _do not I loathe_
> - <sup>j</sup> 24. Or, _way of pain_

## Chapter 140

### Prayer for protection against persecutors.

<sup>1</sup> > _For the Chief Musician. A Psalm of David._Deliver me, O Jehovah, from the evil man;<br>Preserve me from the violent man:<br>
<sup>2</sup> Who devise mischiefs in their heart;<br>Continually do they <sup>a</sup>gather themselves together for war.<br>
<sup>3</sup> They have sharpened their tongue like a serpent;<br>Adders’ poison is under their lips. _[Selah_<br>
<sup>4</sup> Keep me, O Jehovah, from the hands of the wicked;<br>Preserve me from the violent man:<br>Who have purposed to thrust aside my steps.<br>
<sup>5</sup> The proud have hid a snare for me, and cords;<br>They have spread a net by the wayside;<br>They have set gins for me. _[Selah_<br>
<sup>6</sup> I said unto Jehovah, Thou art my God:<br>Give ear unto the voice of my supplications, O Jehovah.<br>
<sup>7</sup> O Jehovah the Lord, the strength of my salvation,<br>Thou hast covered my head in the day of battle.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Grant not, O Jehovah, the desires of the wicked;<br>Further not his evil device, _lest_ they exalt themselves. _[Selah_<br>
<sup>9</sup> As for the head of those that compass me about,<br>Let the mischief of their own lips cover them.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let burning coals fall upon them:<br>Let them be cast into the fire,<br>Into <sup>b</sup>deep pits, whence they shall not rise.<br>
<sup>11</sup> <sup>c</sup>An evil speaker shall not be established in the earth:<br>Evil shall hunt the violent man to overthrow him.<br>
<sup>12</sup> I know that Jehovah will maintain the cause of the afflicted,<br>And justice for the needy.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Surely the righteous shall give thanks unto thy name:<br>The upright shall dwell in thy presence.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _stir up wars_
> - <sup>b</sup> 10. Or, _floods_
> - <sup>c</sup> 11. Hebrew _A man of tongue_.

## Chapter 141

### An evening prayer for sanctification and protection.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Psalm of David._Jehovah, I have called upon thee; make haste unto me:<br>Give ear unto my voice, when I call unto thee.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let my prayer be set forth as incense before thee;<br>The lifting up of my hands as the evening <sup>a</sup>sacrifice.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Set a watch, O Jehovah, before my mouth;<br>Keep the door of my lips.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Incline not my heart to any evil thing,<br>To practise deeds of wickedness<br>With men that work iniquity:<br>And let me not eat of their dainties.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let the righteous smite me, _it shall be_ a kindness;<br>And let him reprove me, _it shall be as_ oil upon the head;<br>Let not my head refuse it:<br>For <sup>b</sup>even in their <sup>c</sup>wickedness shall my prayer continue.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Their judges are thrown down by the sides of the rock;<br>And they shall hear my words; for they are sweet.<br>
<sup>7</sup> As when one ploweth and cleaveth the earth,<br>Our bones are scattered at the mouth of Sheol.<br>
<sup>8</sup> For mine eyes are unto thee, O Jehovah the Lord:<br>In thee do I take refuge; <sup>d</sup>leave not my soul destitute.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Keep me from the snare which they have laid for me,<br>And from the gins of the workers of iniquity.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Let the wicked fall into their own nets,<br>Whilst that I withal <sup>e</sup>escape.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 2. Or, _oblation_
> - <sup>b</sup> 5. Or, _still is my prayer against their wickedness_
> - <sup>c</sup> 5. Or, _calamities_
> - <sup>d</sup> 8. Or, _pour thou not out my life_
> - <sup>e</sup> 10. Hebrew _pass over_.

## Chapter 142

### Prayer for help in trouble.

<sup>1</sup> > _Maschil of David, when he was in the cave; a Prayer._I cry with my voice unto Jehovah;<br>With my voice unto Jehovah do I make supplication.<br>
<sup>2</sup> I pour out my complaint before him;<br>I show before him my trouble.<br>
<sup>3</sup> When my spirit <sup>a</sup>was overwhelmed within me,<br>Thou knewest my path.<br>In the way wherein I walk<br>Have they hidden a snare for me.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Look on _my_ right hand, and see;<br>For there is no man that knoweth me:<br>Refuge hath failed me;<br>No man careth for my soul.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I cried unto thee, O Jehovah;<br>I said, Thou art my refuge,<br>My portion in the land of the living.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Attend unto my cry;<br>For I am brought very low:<br>Deliver me from my persecutors;<br>For they are stronger than I.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Bring my soul out of prison,<br>That I may give thanks unto thy name:<br>The righteous shall <sup>b</sup>compass me about;<br>For thou wilt deal bountifully with me.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 3. Or, _fainted_
> - <sup>b</sup> 7. Or, _crown themselves because of me_

## Chapter 143

### Prayer for deliverance and guidance.

<sup>1</sup> > _A Psalm of David._Hear my prayer, O Jehovah; give ear to my supplications:<br>In thy faithfulness answer me, _and_ in thy righteousness.<br>
<sup>2</sup> And enter not into judgment with thy servant;<br>For in thy sight no man living is righteous.<br>
<sup>3</sup> For the enemy hath persecuted my soul;<br>He hath smitten my life down to the ground:<br>He hath made me to dwell in dark places, as those that have been long dead.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Therefore <sup>a</sup>is my spirit overwhelmed within me;<br>My heart within me is desolate.<br>
<sup>5</sup> I remember the days of old;<br>I meditate on all thy doings;<br>I muse on the work of thy hands.<br>
<sup>6</sup> I spread forth my hands unto thee:<br>My soul _thirsteth_ after thee, as a weary land. _[Selah_<br>
<sup>7</sup> Make haste to answer me, O Jehovah; my spirit faileth:<br>Hide not thy face from me,<br>Lest I become like them that go down into the pit.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Cause me to hear thy lovingkindness in the morning;<br>For in thee do I trust:<br>Cause me to know the way wherein I should walk;<br>For I lift up my soul unto thee.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Deliver me, O Jehovah, from mine enemies:<sup>b</sup><br>I flee unto thee to hide me.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Teach me to do thy will;<br>For thou art my God:<sup>c</sup><br>Thy Spirit is good;<br>Lead me in <sup>d</sup>the land of uprightness.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Quicken me, O Jehovah, for thy name’s sake:<br>In thy righteousness bring my soul out of trouble.<br>
<sup>12</sup> And in thy lovingkindness cut off mine enemies,<br>And destroy all them that afflict my soul;<br>For I am thy servant.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Or, _my spirit fainteth_
> - <sup>b</sup> 9. Hebrew _Unto thee have I hidden_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 10. Or, _Let thy good Spirit lead me_
> - <sup>d</sup> 10. Or, _a plain country_

## Chapter 144

### Prayer for rescue. A happy people described.

<sup>1</sup> > __A Psalm_ of David._Blessed be Jehovah my rock,<br>Who teacheth my hands to war,<br>_And_ my fingers to fight:<br>
<sup>2</sup> My lovingkindness, and my fortress,<br>My high tower, and my deliverer;<br>My shield, and he in whom I take refuge;<br>Who subdueth my people under me.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Jehovah, what is man, that thou takest knowledge of him?<br>Or the son of man, that thou makest account of him?<br>
<sup>4</sup> Man is like to <sup>a</sup>vanity:<br>His days are as a shadow that passeth away.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Bow thy heavens, O Jehovah, and come down:<br>Touch the mountains, and they shall smoke.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Cast forth lightning, and scatter them;<br>Send out thine arrows, and discomfit them.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Stretch forth thy hand from above;<br>Rescue me, and deliver me out of great waters,<br>Out of the hand of aliens;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Whose mouth speaketh deceit,<br>And whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.<br>
<sup>9</sup> I will sing a new song unto thee, O God:<br>Upon a psaltery of ten strings will I sing praises unto thee.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Thou art he that giveth salvation unto kings;<br>Who rescueth David his servant from the hurtful sword.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Rescue me, and deliver me out of the hand of aliens,<br>Whose mouth speaketh deceit,<br>And whose right hand is a right hand of falsehood.<br>
<sup>12</sup> When our sons shall be as plants grown up in their youth,<br>And our daughters as corner-stones hewn after the fashion of a palace;<br>
<sup>13</sup> _When_ our garners are full, affording all manner of store,<br>_And_ our sheep bring forth thousands and ten thousands in our fields;<br>
<sup>14</sup> _When_ our oxen are well laden;<br>_When there is_ no breaking in, and no <sup>b</sup>going forth,<br>And no outcry in our streets:<br>
<sup>15</sup> Happy is the people that is in such a case;<br>_Yea_, happy is the people whose God is Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 4. Hebrew _a breath_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 14. Or, _sallying_

## Chapter 145

### Jehovah extolled for his goodness and power.

<sup>1</sup> > __A Psalm of_ praise; of David._I will extol thee, my God, O King;<br>And I will bless thy name for ever and ever.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Every day will I bless thee;<br>And I will praise thy name for ever and ever.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Great is Jehovah, and greatly to be praised;<br>And his greatness is unsearchable.<br>
<sup>4</sup> One generation shall laud thy works to another,<br>And shall declare thy mighty acts.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Of the glorious majesty of thine honor,<br>And of thy wondrous works, will I meditate.<br>
<sup>6</sup> And men shall speak of the might of thy terrible acts;<br>And I will declare thy greatness.<br>
<sup>7</sup> They shall utter the memory of thy great goodness,<br>And shall sing of thy righteousness.<br>
<sup>8</sup> Jehovah is gracious, and merciful;<br>Slow to anger, and of great lovingkindness.<br>
<sup>9</sup> Jehovah is good to all;<br>And his tender mercies are over all his works.<br>
<sup>10</sup> All thy works shall give thanks unto thee, O Jehovah;<br>And thy saints shall bless thee.<br>
<sup>11</sup> They shall speak of the glory of thy kingdom,<br>And talk of thy power;<br>
<sup>12</sup> To make known to the sons of men his mighty acts,<br>And the glory of the majesty of his kingdom.<br>
<sup>13</sup> Thy kingdom is an everlasting kingdom,<br>And thy dominion _endureth_ throughout all generations.<br>
<sup>14</sup> Jehovah upholdeth all that fall,<br>And raiseth up all those that are bowed down.<br>
<sup>15</sup> The eyes of all wait for thee;<br>And thou givest them their food in due season.<br>
<sup>16</sup> Thou openest thy hand,<br>And <sup>a</sup>satisfiest the desire of every living thing.<br>
<sup>17</sup> Jehovah is righteous in all his ways,<br>And gracious in all his works.<br>
<sup>18</sup> Jehovah is nigh unto all them that call upon him,<br>To all that call upon him in truth.<br>
<sup>19</sup> He will fulfil the desire of them that fear him;<br>He also will hear their cry and will save them.<br>
<sup>20</sup> Jehovah preserveth all them that love him;<br>But all the wicked will he destroy.<br>
<sup>21</sup> My mouth shall speak the praise of Jehovah;<br>And let all flesh bless his holy name for ever and ever.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 16. Or, _satisfiest every living thing with favor_

## Chapter 146

### Praise to Jehovah the abundant Helper.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Praise Jehovah, O my <sup>a</sup>soul.<br>
<sup>2</sup> While I live will I praise Jehovah:<br>I will sing praises unto my God while I have any being.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Put not your trust in princes,<br>Nor in the son of man, in whom there is no help.<br>
<sup>4</sup> His breath goeth forth, he returneth to his earth;<br>In that very day his <sup>b</sup>thoughts perish.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Happy is he that hath the God of Jacob for his help,<br>Whose hope is in Jehovah his God:<br>
<sup>6</sup> Who made heaven and earth,<br>The sea, and all that in them is;<br>Who keepeth truth for ever;<br>
<sup>7</sup> Who executeth justice for the oppressed;<br>Who giveth food to the hungry.<br>Jehovah looseth the prisoners;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Jehovah openeth _the eyes of_ the blind;<br>Jehovah raiseth up them that are bowed down;<br>Jehovah loveth the righteous;<br>
<sup>9</sup> Jehovah preserveth the sojourners;<br>He upholdeth the fatherless and widow;<br>But the way of the wicked he <sup>c</sup>turneth upside down.<br>
<sup>10</sup> Jehovah will reign for ever,<br>Thy God, O Zion, unto all generations.<sup>d</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _purposes_
> - <sup>c</sup> 9. Or, _maketh crooked_
> - <sup>d</sup> 10. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 147

### Praise for Jerusalem’s restoration and prosperity.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah;<br>For it is good to sing praises unto o<sup>a</sup>ur God;<br>For it is ple<sup>b</sup>asant, _and_ praise is comely.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Jehovah doth build up Jerusalem;<br>He gathereth together the outcasts of Israel.<br>
<sup>3</sup> He healeth the broken in heart,<br>And bindeth up their <sup>c</sup>wounds.<br>
<sup>4</sup> He counteth the number of the stars;<br>He calleth them all by _their_ names.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Great is our Lord, and mighty in power;<br>His understanding is infinite.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Jehovah upholdeth the meek:<br>He bringeth the wicked down to the ground.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Sing unto Jehovah with thanksgiving;<br>Sing praises upon the harp unto our God,<br>
<sup>8</sup> Who covereth the heavens with clouds,<br>Who prepareth rain for the earth,<br>Who maketh grass to grow upon the mountains.<br>
<sup>9</sup> He giveth to the beast his food,<br>_And_ to the young ravens which cry.<br>
<sup>10</sup> He delighteth not in the strength of the horse:<br>He taketh no pleasure in the legs of a man.<br>
<sup>11</sup> Jehovah taketh pleasure in them that fear him,<br>In those that hope in his lovingkindness.<br>
<sup>12</sup> Praise Jehovah, O Jerusalem;<br>Praise thy God, O Zion.<br>
<sup>13</sup> For he hath strengthened the bars of thy gates;<br>He hath blessed thy children within thee.<br>
<sup>14</sup> He maketh <sup>d</sup>peace in thy borders;<br>He filleth thee with the <sup>e</sup>finest of the wheat.<br>
<sup>15</sup> He sendeth out his commandment upon earth;<br>His word runneth very swiftly.<br>
<sup>16</sup> He giveth snow like wool;<br>He scattereth the hoar-frost like ashes.<br>
<sup>17</sup> He casteth forth his ice like morsels:<br>Who can stand before his cold?<br>
<sup>18</sup> He sendeth out his word, and melteth them:<br>He causeth his wind to blow, and the waters flow.<br>
<sup>19</sup> He showeth his word unto Jacob,<br>His statutes and his ordinances unto Israel.<br>
<sup>20</sup> He hath not dealt so with any nation;<br>And as for his ordinances, they have not known them.<sup>f</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 1. Or, _For he is good: sing praises unto our God; For he is gracious_
> - <sup>c</sup> 3. Hebrew _sorrows_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 14. Hebrew _thy border peace_.
> - <sup>e</sup> 14. Hebrew _fat of wheat_.
> - <sup>f</sup> 20. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 148

### The whole Creation invoked to praise Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Praise ye Jehovah from the h<sup>a</sup>eavens:<br>Praise him in the heights.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Praise ye him, all his angels:<br>Praise ye him, all his host.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise ye him, sun and moon:<br>Praise him, all ye stars of light.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Praise him, ye heavens of heavens,<br>And ye waters that are above the heavens.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let them praise the name of Jehovah;<br>For he commanded, and they were created.<br>
<sup>6</sup> He hath also established them for ever and ever:<br>He hath made a decree <sup>b</sup>which shall not pass away.<br>
<sup>7</sup> Praise Jehovah from the earth,<br>Ye sea-monsters, and all deeps;<br>
<sup>8</sup> Fire and hail, snow and vapor;<br>Stormy wind, fulfilling his word;<br>
<sup>9</sup> Mountains and all hills;<br>Fruitful trees and all cedars;<br>
<sup>10</sup> Beasts and all cattle;<br>Creeping things and flying birds;<br>
<sup>11</sup> Kings of the earth and all peoples;<br>Princes and all judges of the earth;<br>
<sup>12</sup> Both young men and virgins;<br>Old men and children:<br>
<sup>13</sup> Let them praise the name of Jehovah;<br>For his name alone is exalted;<br>His glory is above the earth and the heavens.<br>
<sup>14</sup> And he hath lifted up <sup>c</sup>the horn of his people,<br>The praise of all his saints;<br>Even of the children of Israel, a people near unto him.<sup>d</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Or, _which none shall transgress_
> - <sup>c</sup> 14. Or, _a horn for his people, A praise for all his saints; Even for etc._
> - <sup>d</sup> 14. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 149

### Israel invoked to praise Jehovah.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Sing unto Jehov<sup>a</sup>ah a new song,<br>And his praise in the assembly of the saints.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Let Israel rejoice in him that made him:<br>Let the children of Zion be joyful in their King.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Let them praise his name in the dance:<br>Let them sing praises unto him with timbrel and harp.<br>
<sup>4</sup> For Jehovah taketh pleasure in his people:<br>He will beautify the meek with <sup>b</sup>salvation.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Let the saints exult in glory:<br>Let them sing for joy upon their beds.<br>
<sup>6</sup> _Let_ the high praises of God _be_ in their <sup>c</sup>mouth,<br>And a two-edged sword in their hand;<br>
<sup>7</sup> To execute vengeance upon the nations,<br>And punishments upon the peoples;<br>
<sup>8</sup> To bind their kings with chains,<br>And their nobles with fetters of iron;<br>
<sup>9</sup> To execute upon them the judgment written:<sup>d</sup><br>This honor have all his saints.<sup>e</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 4. Or, _victory_
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Hebrew _throat_.
> - <sup>d</sup> 9. Or, _He is the honor of all his saints_
> - <sup>e</sup> 9. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.

## Chapter 150

### Call to praise God with musical instruments.

<sup>1</sup> Praise ye Jehovah.<br>Praise God in his sanctuar<sup>a</sup>y:<br>Praise him in the firmament of his power.<br>
<sup>2</sup> Praise him for his mighty acts:<br>Praise him according to his excellent greatness.<br>
<sup>3</sup> Praise him with trumpet sound:<br>Praise him with psaltery and harp.<br>
<sup>4</sup> Praise him with timbrel and dance:<br>Praise him with stringed instruments and pipe.<br>
<sup>5</sup> Praise him with loud cymbals:<br>Praise him with high sounding cymbals.<br>
<sup>6</sup> Let everything that hath breath praise <sup>b</sup>Jehovah.<sup>c</sup><br>Praise ye Jehovah.<br>

> - <sup>a</sup> 1. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
> - <sup>b</sup> 6. Hebrew _Jah_.
> - <sup>c</sup> 6. Hebrew _Hallelujah_.
